# Third-party licenses

CantiniAutoGPS itself is released under the MIT License (see `LICENSE`).

It includes and links the third-party software below. Each component remains under its own license; the notices required by those licenses are reproduced here.

## Bundled: ExifTool

ExifTool 13.55 by Phil Harvey (https://exiftool.org) is bundled unmodified in `vendor/exiftool/` (apart from removing documentation files and setting the shebang line). It is distributed under the same terms as Perl: the Artistic License or the GNU GPL. It is redistributed here under the Artistic License; see `vendor/exiftool/LICENSE-Artistic.txt`. The complete ExifTool source is the set of Perl files in `vendor/exiftool/`.

## Fonts

The binary embeds egui's default fonts (Hack, Ubuntu-Light, Noto Emoji, emoji-icon-font), whose licenses (MIT, Ubuntu Font Licence 1.0, SIL OFL 1.1) are included below under `epaint_default_fonts`. On macOS the app also *uses* the system font (San Francisco) at runtime; that font is not redistributed.

## Crates without a separate license file

These crates declare their license in `Cargo.toml` but ship no license file in their published package. They are used under the declared license; the text of those licenses (MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD, Zlib, BSL-1.0, ...) is the standard text reproduced above for other crates. Authors and repositories are taken from each crate's `Cargo.toml`.
